# Agents · A · part 3

944 entries, acceptance-tester to account-executive. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **acceptance-tester**: Run the card's human-authored acceptance criteria against the branch and return a per-criterion verdict. Used by the Code flow's `acceptance` node after smoke passes; the card ref arrives in the message. Returns pass/fail/blocked plus a per-criterion checklist. ([lean-software-production/ensembleworks](https://github.com/lean-software-production/ensembleworks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acceptance-tester.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **acceptance-tester**: PlanGate V-1 受け入れ検査エージェント。test-cases.md の完了条件を1つずつ機械的に突合し、PASS/FAIL を判定する。推測ではなく実行結果のみで判定する。 ([s977043/PlanGate](https://github.com/s977043/PlanGate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acceptance-tester.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **acceptance-tester**: dddjango 파이프라인 Phase 2(구현) 시작에 Coordinator가 호출한다(G1 전에는 `PHASE1_ARRANGE_CHECK` 읽기 전용 점검으로만 호출한다). 승인된 현행 외부 계약과 관련 테스트를 대조하고, 입장 표에서 `add/update`로 승인된 외부 계약만 바깥 루프 Red로 만든다. 구현을 보지 않는 블랙박스다. 구현 코드는 쓰지 않는다. ([changja88/dddjango](https://github.com/changja88/dddjango/blob/HEAD/dddjango/agents/acceptance-tester.md) · ★1)
- **acceptance-tester**: Acceptance Tester — Validate implementation against Spec (spawn on demand) ([fulcrumaxe/fulcrumaxe](https://github.com/fulcrumaxe/fulcrumaxe/blob/HEAD/agents/acceptance-tester.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **acceptance-tester**: Use at the end of a milestone (M0–M6 in `docs/development-plan.md`) to verify it actually meets its acceptance criteria. Reads the milestone's "Acceptance" section, runs every programmatic step, captures the output, and produces a pass/fail report per step. For UI steps that can't be programmatically verified, emits a manual verification checklist. Use proactively when the human or main loop is about to mark a milestone complete — never trust a "done" claim without running this first. ([GeekLad/jup-lend-rebalancer](https://github.com/GeekLad/jup-lend-rebalancer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acceptance-tester.md) · ★1)
- **acceptance-tester**: Objective acceptance testing from isolated context. Acts as product owner proxy, manually verifying each criterion. Use when story is ready for acceptance testing. ([kecbigmt/agent-plugins](https://github.com/kecbigmt/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/story-driven/agents/acceptance-tester.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **acceptance-tester**: Use when you need to verify delivered behavior against agreed product criteria. ([crankyjediii/claude-code-subagents](https://github.com/crankyjediii/claude-code-subagents/blob/HEAD/categories/08-business-product/agents/acceptance-tester.md))
- **acceptance-tester**: Plugin-level acceptance-tester agent for loom-code. Dispatched fresh-context by the closing-review station to build and run the change in a clean environment and walk every Acceptance line of the intent, producing docs/loom/&lt;change-id&gt;/acceptance-test-report.md — the document the user reads to accept the change. Never an agent that implemented it. Reusable cross-plugin via subagent_type "loom-code:acceptance-tester". ([kouko/loom-plugins](https://github.com/kouko/loom-plugins/blob/HEAD/loom-code/agents/acceptance-tester.md))
- **acceptance-tester**: Writes behavior tests from a plan's acceptance examples, in its own worktree, without ever reading the implementation. ([thatsnotmynameio/crew](https://github.com/thatsnotmynameio/crew/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/acceptance-tester.md))
- **acceptance-testing**: Two layers provide separate evidence. Map each issue acceptance criterion to stable IDs and named executable tests in the issue's verification record. - **acceptance-validator**: Validate that a Beta phase delivered what the plan said and that the written record matches what the code does — scope coverage, registered-but-dead surface, deviations, and contradictions against earlier phase write-ups. Comprueba según WCAG 2.2 nivel AA en las áreas que más importan para sitios pequeños, corrige automáticamente lo que es seguro corregir y lista lo que requiere una decisión humana. Úsalo antes del lanzamiento o antes de presentar a clientes que exijan accesibilidad. - **Acción 01 — Investigar Requerimiento**: Pipeline action 01/17. Use to analyze a new product idea or requirement from a business and user perspective before any technical work begins. Produces a deep understanding of the problem: who has the problem, why they have it, what they're doing today instead, and what success looks like. Triggers on: "acción 01", "investigar requerimiento", "action 01", "investigate requirement", "entender el problema", "contexto del req". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.investigar-requerimiento.agent.md))
- **Acción 02 — Análisis Funcional**: Pipeline action 02/17. Use to define the complete functional scope of the product: what it does, what it doesn't do, key user flows, and functional requirements with acceptance criteria. Builds on the investigation from Action 01. Triggers on: "acción 02", "análisis funcional", "action 02", "functional analysis", "scope funcional", "qué hace el sistema", "functional requirements", "casos de uso". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.analisis-funcional.agent.md))
- **Acción 03 — Revisión Técnica del Requerimiento**: Pipeline action 03/17. Use to validate that the requirement is technically sound, identify technical unknowns and risks early, and confirm that the proposed stack (.NET 8, n8n, Gemini, Azure SQL, Next.js) is appropriate for the functional scope. Triggers on: "acción 03", "revisión técnica", "action 03", "technical review", "viabilidad inicial", "stack apropiado", "technical feasibility of requirement". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.revision-tecnica-req.agent.md))
- **Acción 04 — Viabilidad Técnica**: Pipeline action 04/17. Use to produce a comprehensive technical feasibility assessment covering architecture fit, integration complexity, performance expectations, estimated technical effort, and a final viability verdict with level of confidence. Builds on Action 03 red flags. Triggers on: "acción 04", "viabilidad técnica", "action 04", "technical feasibility", "factibilidad técnica", "es técnicamente viable", "cuánto cuesta técnicamente", "technical viability report". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.viabilidad-tecnica.agent.md))
- **Acción 05 — Riesgos e Impacto**: Pipeline action 05/17. Use to identify, classify, and plan mitigation for all technical and business risks of the project. Also assesses impact on existing systems, teams, and processes. Triggers on: "acción 05", "riesgos", "action 05", "risk analysis", "análisis de riesgos", "impacto del sistema", "risk register", "risk matrix", "riesgos técnicos", "riesgos de negocio". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.riesgos-impacto.agent.md))
- **Acción 06 — Shape Up del Alcance**: Pipeline action 06/17. Use to apply the Shape Up methodology to define the project appetite, carve the scope into bets with fixed time, and identify the rabbit holes to avoid. Produces a shaped pitch ready for the betting table. Triggers on: "acción 06", "shape up", "action 06", "shaping", "apetito", "appetite", "pitch del proyecto", "scope fijo", "fixed time variable scope", "betting table". Use to define the MVP strategy: what the minimum viable product includes, the value hypothesis, positioning against alternatives, and the success metrics to validate the MVP. - **Acción 08 — Diseño y Prototipo del MVP**: Pipeline action 08/17. Use to define the UX/UI design of the MVP: user flows, wireframe-level descriptions, design system decisions, and a clickable prototype concept. Triggers on: "acción 08", "diseño del MVP", "action 08", "MVP design", "prototipo UX", "flujos de usuario", "wireframes", "diseño de la interfaz", "user interface design", "design prototype". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.diseno-prototipo-mvp.agent.md))
- **Acción 09 — Arquitectura de la Solución**: Pipeline action 09/17. Use to design the complete solution architecture: component diagram, data flow, API contracts, database schema additions, and infrastructure topology. Produces Architecture Decision Records (ADRs) for key decisions. Triggers on: "acción 09", "arquitectura", "action 09", "solution architecture", "component diagram", "data flow", "API design", "architecture decision", "ADR", "diseño de arquitectura", "diagrama de componentes". - **Acción 10 — Revisión de Seguridad**: Pipeline action 10/17. Use to review the proposed architecture and implementation plan against OWASP Top 10, identify security risks specific to AI/LLM systems (prompt injection, data leakage), and produce a security checklist and hardening plan. Triggers on: "acción 10", "revisión de seguridad", "action 10", "security review", "OWASP", "prompt injection", "seguridad del chatbot", "vulnerabilidades", "hardening", "security audit", "LLM security". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.revision-seguridad.agent.md))
- **Acción 11 — Infraestructura Azure**: Pipeline action 11/17. Triggers on: "acción 11", "infraestructura", "action 11", "Azure infrastructure", "recursos Azure", "App Service", "Key Vault setup", "Terraform Azure", "IaC", "infraestructura como código", "Azure deployment", "configuración Azure". - **Acción 12 — Historias de Usuario**: Pipeline action 12/17. Triggers on: "acción 12", "historias de usuario", "action 12", "user stories", "acceptance criteria", "Given When Then", "BDD", "INVEST", "criterios de aceptación", "backlog del MVP". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.historias-usuario.agent.md))
- **Acción 13 — Estrategia de QA**: Pipeline action 13/17.
